Function logit_score(z, n, N, tau):

    fa = rfft(a,N)

Weighted deterministic swap:

- Compute cross array length n via FFT of a=b and p_rev. But for deterministic linearized swap delta, we need cross_i = ∑ p_k b_{k-i} (same grad). We can compute G=2 cross; val_i = 2 cross_i + p[2*i] (since p length 2n-1, 2i up to 2n-2). For remove choose max val over ones? Let's derive sign carefully.

Let q=c·p = ∑ p_k c_k. Delta for swap remove i (1->0) and add j (0->1): b' = b - e_i + e_j.
q' = q + q(e_j) - q(e_i) +? since b_i=1,b_j=0:
δq = ∑ p_k [(b+δ)^2 - b^2] = 2∑ b_k p_{?} + self.
Add at j:
- new pairs e_j with old b: 2∑_t b_t p_{t+j}, and self e_j e_j: p_{2j}.
Remove at i:
- old pairs e_i with b: 2∑_t b_t p_{t+i}, and self p_{2i}.
So δq = 2∑_t b_t(p_{t+j}-p_{t+i}) + p_{2j} - p_{2i}.
Cross_j = ∑_t b_t p_{j+t}? Our cross_i from grad = ∑_k p_k b_{k-i} = ∑_t b_t p_{t+i}. Yes. So δq = 2(cross_j - cross_i) + p2j - p2i.
We want decrease q. Since q approximates max (or smoothed objective). For fixed i, best j zeros minimizes 2cross_j + p2j. For fixed j, best i ones maximizes 2cross_i + p2i. Thus candidate: i = argmax val over ones, j=argmin val over zeros. If val_i == val_j? gradient zero.

To avoid cycles, if exact max improves accept; else maybe accept random small delta (SA). Deterministic descending swaps can quickly converge but get stuck. We can also select among top K by val not just one:

- Addition set A: top Amax from zeros ascending val plus random zeros.
- Pairwise or k combinations; evaluate exact score after update. Maintain best candidate.
- If no improvement, perform simulated annealing: accept moves with positive delta in smooth q if q_try<q_cur or prob exp(-Δ/ T). But exact objective is max; q smooth. Maybe structure SA loop:
- Outer loop compute weights p and val every W iterations (e.g., every 32 or 64 swaps) or when stale.
- Inner make one or k-swap moves:

  - Evaluate exact score (or q) for candidate. If improvement, accept and perhaps reset stale. If not, accept with some probability if q_delta small/random.
- Temperature decrease. Diversify by random resets or local rewrites.

def remove_one(b,c,i):
    c[i:i+n] -= 2.0*b
    c[2*i] += 1.0
    b[i] = 0.0

def add_one(b,c,j):
    c[j:j+n] += 2.0*b
    c[2*j] += 1.0
    b[j] = 1.0
```

For kswap:
```
def apply_kswap(b,c,removes,adds,n):
   for i in removes:
       assert b[i]>0.5
       c[i:i+n] -= 2.0*b
       c[2*i] += 1.0
       b[i]=0.0
   for j in adds:
       assert b[j]<0.5
       c[j:j+n] += 2.0*b
       c[2*j] += 1.0
       b[j]=1.0

def revert_kswap(b,c,removes,adds,n):
   for j in reversed(adds):
       c[j:j+n] -= 2.0*b
       c[2*j] += 1.0
       b[j]=0.0
   for i in reversed(removes):
       c[i:i+n] += 2.0*b
       c[2*i] += 1.0
       b[i]=1.0
```
Check revert add_i: b currently has i=0; add uses b with all original except adds removed and i 0; good. The update uses `b` current before setting. For add, no need to ensure j=0? It is. Good.

But in apply_kswap, if `adds` includes multiple, after first add, b[j1]=1; second add uses b including j1, which is correct: new support includes previous added. Good.

```
def choose_by_score(vals, pick_max, temp, rng):
   vals=vals[finite]
   if pick_max:
       z=(vals - np.max(vals))/max(temp*range,1e-9)
   else:
       z=(np.min(vals)-vals)/max(temp*range,1e-9)
   clip; exp; normalize; return rng.choice

But we can do better: deterministic best single using val and exact; if reject, choose next-best swaps among top K (e.g., 10) until accept or stale. This avoids evaluating all combinations repeatedly.

Let's outline `optimize_binary` algorithm in detail:
```
def optimize_binary(b0, deadline, seed, m_override=None, verbose=False):
    rng = default_rng(seed)
    b = b0.copy()
    n=b.size
    m = int(round(b.sum())) if override None else override
    if m != count: adjust b to m by random removals/additions? use binary_from_values
    c = rint(convolve_seq(b, next_pow2(2*n-1))).astype(float)
    best_max = max(c); best_score=2n*best_max/(m*m); best_b=b.copy()
    Np2 = next_pow2(3*n-2)
    beta = 0.25
    temp = 0.01 # score temp? Actually score delta small.
    stale = 0; no_best=0; move_count=0; last_best=best_score
    while time < deadline:
        cmax = max(c)
        if cmax < best_max: update best
        score_current = 2*n*cmax/(m*m)
        tau = max(beta*cmax, 1e-12)
        z=(c-cmax)/tau; z=np.clip(z,-40,0); ez=np.exp(z); s=ez.sum(); if finite: p=ez/s
        # maybe floor p: p = (1-floor)*p + floor/L
        f_b = rfft(b, Np2)
        p_rev = p[::-1]
        f_p = rfft(p_rev, Np2)
        cross = irfft(f_b*f_p, Np2)[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
        p2 = p[0:2*n-1:2]
        val = 2*cross + p2
        # maybe center val? Not necessary.
        # Try up to batch=16 moves without recomputing val.
        batch=0; moved_since_recompute=0
        while batch < batch_size and time<deadline:
            ones = np.flatnonzero(b>0.5)
            zeros = np.flatnonzero(b<0.5)
            if empty break
            # choose candidate(i,j)
            if stale>30 or rng.random()<0.2:
                i = ones[rng.integers(len(ones))]
                j = zeros[rng.integers(len(zeros))]
            else:
                # top deterministic with noise among top ranks
                top_k = 20
                v1 = val[ones]; v0=val[zeros]
                # select indices with highest v1 (remove) and lowest v0 (add)
                # To avoid repeated, choose randomly with probabilities decaying by rank
                order1 = np.argpartition(-v1, top_k)[:top_k]
                order0 = np.argpartition(v0, top_k)[:top_k]
                # use exponential weights over ranks maybe
                rank1 = ...; choose i
                rank0...
            if i==j: continue
            # apply swap single (remove i then add j)
            apply_remove(b,c,i)
            apply_add(b,c,j)
            cmax_new=max(c); score_new=2n*cmax_new/m2
            # acceptance
            if score_new < score_current*(1-1e-12):
                accept
            else:
                delta=score_new-score_current
                # delta small maybe; T in score units; start 0.002? Score changes ~0.000? For n=8192, one coefficient change up to 2n/m2 ~8192*? /? Actually max coefficient change up to n; score change =2n*maxchange/m2 ~ (2n/m2)*change. For m=n/2, factor=2/n? Wait m2=n^2/4, 2n/m2=8/n=0.001 for n=8192. Coef change <=n => score change<=1. Usually 0.001-0.01. T=0.002 ok.
                accept = rng.random() < exp(-delta/temp) if delta<temp*5 else False
            if accept:
                score_current=score_new
                if score_new<best_score: update best
                stale=0; beta=max(0.08,beta*0.99); temp=min(0.02? temp*1.05?)
                moved_since_recompute+=1
            else:
                revert_remove? Actually forward was remove i, add j. To revert: remove j (current 1), add i (current 0):
                apply_remove(b,c,j)
                apply_add(b,c,i)
                stale+=1
                temp*=0.97
                if stale % 200 ==0: beta=min(0.8,beta*1.5); temp=min(0.03,temp*1.7); stale=0
            batch +=1
            if moved_since_recompute >= recalc_after_accept or stale >= recalc_after_stale: break
        # maybe occasional k-swap if stale high
        if stale > 100:
            try 2-swap candidate: choose top 20 ones by val high, zeros low; for 100 random combos apply two removes/adds, if improvement accept; if none, random SA 2swap.
            stale=0
    return normalize_positive(best_b,0), best_score
```

Need ensure `apply_remove` and `apply_add` are top-level with n? They need n from b.size. Maybe pass b,c,n. We can implement:
```

Candidate selection by rank:
```
def choose_ranked(vals, pick_high, rng, top=30, temp=0.3):
   order_all? For efficiency top via argpartition if len vals >top.
   if pick_high:
       if size<=top: weights=exp(-z) maybe; but we want top high distribute.
       part = np.argpartition(-vals, top)[:top]
       v = vals[part]
       # rank weights: assign positions sorted descending
       sorted_vals = np.sort(v)[::-1]
       w = np.exp(-0.35*np.arange(len(sorted_vals))/temp?) But need map to part. Simpler zipf weights: w = 1/(rank+1) after sorting v descending.
   else (low): similar.
   return rng.choice(part, p=weights/weights.sum())
```

Adam details:
```
def value_grad_z(z, n, N, tau):
    a = softmax(z) # maybe use stable, sum1
    fa=rfft(a,N); c=irfft(fa*fa,N)[:2n-1]
    cmax=max(c)
    # S = cmax + tau * logsumexp((c-cmax)/tau)
    if tau <=0: S=cmax; p = indicator max
    else: shifted=(c-cmax)/tau; clip(-40,0); ez=exp; s=sum; S=cmax+tau*log(s)
    score=2n*S
    # gradient wrt z:
    p=ez/s (or if tau=0 choose uniform over max coeffs)
    p_rev = p[::-1]
    fw=rfft(p_rev,N)
    cross=irfft(fa*fw,N)[n-1:2n-1][::-1]
    G = 4.0*n*cross  # dJ/da
    grad_z = a*(G - a.dot(G))
    return score, grad_z
```
Adam:
- z = log(a+1e-12); z-=mean
- momentum m=0, v=0
- lr_start maybe 0.02? Need gradient units ~0.001. Adam step size ~lr. Try lr=0.05, 0.02. If too large, objective worsens; can line-search? We can use adaptive: after compute grad, try step with decay if fail.
Simplest: use RMSProp/Adam no constraint; clip z to [-20,20] maybe. But if a has zeros, log small. We'll initial `a_eps = max(a, 1e-12); a_eps/=sum; z=log(a_eps) - mean`.
Loop:
```
for it in range(maxit):
   value, g = value_grad
   if value<best: update
   # normalize g? sqrt(sum(g^2)) maybe. Adam handles.
   m = b1*m+(1-b1)*g; v=b2*v+(1-b2)*g*g
   mhat=m/(1-b1^t); vhat=v/(1-b2^t)
   step = lr * mhat/(sqrt(vhat)+eps)
   z -= step (minimize)
   z -= mean(z)
   z = clip(z,-30,30)
   # optional line search: if new value > old, revert and reduce lr
```

```
old_z=z; new_value? We can compute value after step; if new_value > value*(1+0.01): revert z; lr*=0.7 else lr=min(max,l rp*1.05)
```

Let's detail helper functions.

`next_pow2`: as before.

`normalize_positive`:
```
def normalize_positive(a, floor=0.0):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size==0: return np.ones(1)
    if floor>0: a=np.maximum(a,float(floor)) else a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
    s=float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s<=1e-300: return np.full(a.size,1.0/a.size)
    return a/s
```

`convolve_full`:
```
def convolve_full(a, N=None):
    a=np.asarray(a,float); n=a.size
    if n==0: return np.array([])
    if N is None: N=next_pow2(2*n-1)
    else: N=max(N,next_pow2(2*n-1))
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    return np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,N)[:2*n-1].astype(np.float64, copy=False)
```
For binary exact, use `np.rint` but not needed. In score_binary, `c=convolve_full(b); cmax=float(np.max(c))` For binary direct FFT may produce small errors e.g., 1023.999999; `np.max` maybe. For binary local c maintained exact; for raw score we can use direct integer? For raw generation many candidates, could use `np.rint(convolve_full(b))` to get integer counts. Since b 0/1, FFT errors ~1e-10, rint okay. Then cmax int. `m=b.sum()`.

`internal_score`:
```
def internal_score(a, N=None):
    a=normalize_positive(a)
    if a.size: s=1; c=convolve_full(a,N); cmax=float(np.max(c)); return 2*n*cmax
```
But if a has floor? Yes.

`softmax_seq`:
```
def softmax_seq(z):
    z=np.asarray(z,float); maxz=float(np.max(z)); z=z-maxz; z=np.clip(z,-60,0); e=np.exp(z); e=np.maximum(e,1e-300); s=e.sum(); if not finite return uniform; return e/s
```

`score_binary(b)`:
```
def binary_score(b):
    b=np.asarray(b,float).ravel(); n=b.size
    m=float(np.rint(b.sum()))? If b not exact, use sum.
    if m<=0: return inf, 0
    c=convolve_full(b, next_pow2(2*n-1))
    cmax=float(np.max(c))
    # if near integer, use cmax? no.
    return float(2.0*n*cmax/(m*m)), cmax
```
If using b with m integer. For normalized values, sum maybe 1; internal_score okay.

`is_prime` and `next_prime` simple. For performance, small primes list. Up to 32768. Use trial division odd.
```
def is_prime(x):
    x=int(x)
    if x<2:return False
    if x%2==0:return x==2
    if x%3==0:return x==3
    d=5; w=2; while d*d<=x: if x%d==0 return False; d+=w; w=6-d
    return True
```

`qr_binary(p, include_zero=True)`:
```
x=np.arange(p,dtype=np.int64)
idx=(x*x)%p
b=np.zeros(p,float); b[idx]=1.0
if not include_zero: b[0]=0.0
return b
```
If p large x*x within int64. Good.

Now `optimize_binary` implementation in detail.

```
def optimize_binary(b0, deadline, seed=0, m_override=None, verbose=False):
    b=np.asarray(b0,float).copy()
    n=b.size
    b = b.astype(float)
    if m_override is not None:
       m=int(m_override)
       # set count
       order=np.argpartition(-b, max(1,m-1))[:m]
       b2=np.zeros(n); b2[order]=1.0; b=b2
    else:
       m=int(np.rint(float(b.sum())))
       if m==0: m=n//2; b[...]=...
    # ensure value nonneg: b=np.where(b>0.5,1,0)
    c=np.rint(convolve_full(b, next_pow2(2*n-1))).astype(float)
    best_max=float(np.max(c)); best_score=2*n*best_max/(m*m); best_b=b.copy()
    N=next_pow2(3*n-2)
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    beta=0.35
    temp=0.005
    stale=0; eval_count=0; start_best=best_score
    batch=0
    # local variables for speed
    two=2.0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        # recompute weights
        cmax=float(np.max(c))
        if cmax < best_max:
            best_max=cmax; best_score=2*n*best_max/(m*m); best_b=b.copy()
            if verbose: print(...)
        if cmax<=0: break
        score_current=2*n*cmax/(m*m)
        tau=beta*max(cmax,1e-12)
        z=(c-cmax)/tau
        np.clip(z, -40.0, 0.0, out=z)
        ez=np.exp(z)
        s=float(ez.sum())
        if not finite or s<=0: p = uniform? else p=ez/s
        # p floor
        p = 0.98*p + 0.02/float(2*n-1)
        # FFT
        fb=np.fft.rfft(b, N)
        p_rev=p[::-1]
        fp=np.fft.rfft(p_rev, N)
        cross=np.fft.irfft(fb*fp, N)[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
        p2=p[0:2*n-1:2]
        val=2.0*cross + p2
        # maybe val for zeros and ones within arrays stale?
        # batch loop
        batch=0
        max_batch=64 if n<8192 else 24? Larger n fewer.
        while batch < max_batch and time.monotonic() < deadline:
            ones=np.flatnonzero(b>0.5)
            zeros=np.flatnonzero(b<0.5)
            if ones.size==0 or zeros.size==0: break
            # choose i,j
            if stale > 400 or rng.random() < 0.15:
                i=int(ones[rng.integers(ones.size)])
                j=int(zeros[rng.integers(zeros.size)])
            else:
                # deterministic or ranked with noise
                if stale > 20:
                    noise = 0.25 * max(1e-12, float(np.ptp(val))) * (stale/20.0)
                    vone = val[ones] + rng.normal(0.0, noise, ones.size)
                    vzero = val[zeros] + rng.normal(0.0, noise, zeros.size)
                else:
                    vone = val[ones]; vzero=val[zeros]
                i = int(ones[int(np.argmax(vone))])
                j = int(zeros[int(np.argmin(vzero))])
            if i == j: continue
            # apply
            c[i:i+n] -= 2.0*b
            c[2*i] += 1.0
            b[i]=0.0
            c[j:j+n] += 2.0*b
            c[2*j] += 1.0
            b[j]=1.0
            cmax_new=float(np.max(c))
            score_new=2*n*cmax_new/(m*m)
            if score_new < score_current*(1.0 - 1e-12) or (score_new < best_score and rng.random()<0.1?):
                accept=True
            else:
                delta=score_new-score_current
                if delta <= 1e-13: accept = rng.random()<0.3
                else: accept = rng.random() < np.exp(-delta/temp) if delta < 5*temp else False
            if accept:
                score_current=score_new
                if score_new < best_score:
                    best_score=score_new; best_max=cmax_new; best_b=b.copy()
                    if verbose: print...
                stale=0
                beta=max(0.07, beta*0.985)
                temp=min(0.02, temp*1.03)
                batch=max(0,batch-2)? maybe continue
            else:
                # revert
                c[j:j+n] -= 2.0*b   # b currently has i=0,j=1
                c[2*j] += 1.0
                b[j]=0.0
                c[i:i+n] += 2.0*b
                c[2*i] += 1.0
                b[i]=1.0
                stale += 1
                temp=max(1e-5, temp*0.97)
                if stale % 100 ==0:
                    beta=min(1.2, beta*1.35)
                    temp=min(0.05, temp*1.5)
                    stale=0
            batch += 1
            if score_current < 1.15: # very good stop?
                # maybe continue with smaller? but break to save time
                # We can break if below 1.15, but further reward maybe continue.
                pass
        # if stale high after batch, maybe kswap
        if stale > 0 and time... do? Maybe skip.
        if verbose and eval_count%10... print
```

Implement top selection helper:
```
def top_indices(vals, size, pick_high=True, rng=None):
    size=min(int(size), len(vals))
    if size<=0: return np.array([],int)
    if pick_high:
        idx=np.argpartition(-vals, size-1)[:size] if len(vals)>size else np.argsort(-vals)
    else:
        idx=np.argpartition(vals, size-1)[:size] if len(vals)>size else np.argsort(vals)
    return idx
```

`logit_score_grad` top-level:
```
def logit_score_grad(z, n, N, tau):
    a=softmax_seq(z)
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,N)[:2*n-1]
    cmax=float(np.max(c))
    if tau <= 0:
       # choose p uniform over max coefficients for grad
       mask=np.isclose(c,cmax,rtol=1e-12,atol=1e-15)
       p=np.where(mask,1/mask.sum(),0)
       S=cmax
    else:
       shifted=(c-cmax)/tau; np.clip(shifted,-40,0); ez=np.exp(shifted); s=sum
       p=ez/s
       S=cmax+tau*np.log(s)
    score=float(2*n*S)
    p_rev=p[::-1]
    fw=np.fft.rfft(p_rev,N)
    cross=np.fft.irfft(fa*fw,N)[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
    G=4.0*n*cross
    grad_z=a*(G - float(a.dot(G)))
    return score, grad_z
```
For value only (with L-BFGS) we'd need separate but for Adam use combined.

Let's detail `apply_kswap` and `revert_kswap` maybe not used if no 2-swap. But include for potential. They use n = b.size? avoid passing.
```
def apply_kswap(b,c,removes,adds):
    n=b.size
    for i in removes:
        if b[i] > 0.5:
            c[i:i+n] -= 2.0*b
            c[2*i] += 1.0
            b[i]=0.0
    for j in adds:
        if b[j] < 0.5:
            c[j:j+n] += 2.0*b
            c[2*j] += 1.0
            b[j]=1.0
```
If removes length zero okay.
Revert:
```
def revert_kswap(b,c,removes,adds):
    n=b.size
    for j in reversed(adds):
       if b[j]>0.5:
          c[j:j+n]-=2.0*b
          c[2*j]+=1.0
          b[j]=0.0
    for i in reversed(removes):
       if b[i]<0.5:
          c[i:i+n]+=2.0*b
          c[2*i]+=1.0
          b[i]=1.0
```
Python `reversed(adds)` if adds tuple/list. We'll pass tuples.

Do we need 2-swap? We can implement within optimize using apply_kswap. But to keep code manageable, maybe include simple 2-swap when stale high.

Implement helper `pick_two_unique(pool, rng)`:
```
def pick_two_unique(pool, rng):
    m=pool.size
    if m<2: return -1,-1
    a=int(rng.integers(m))
    b=int(rng.integers(m-1))
    if b>=a: b+=1
    return int(pool[a]), int(pool[b])
```

    b = np.asarray(b0, dtype=float).ravel().copy()
    n = b.size
    m = int(np.rint(float(np.sum(b))))
    if m <= 0:
       m = max(2,n//2); b[top?]...

    b = np.where(b>0.5, 1.0, 0.0)
    if int(np.rint(b.sum())) != m:
       # adjust
       cur=int(b.sum()); if cur>m: turn off largest? Use top_m_binary from ones? Could simply b=make_random_binary(n, m/n, rng) if mismatch.
    c = np.rint(convolve_full(b, next_pow2(2*n-1))).astype(float)
    best_max=float(np.max(c)); best_score=2*n*best_max/(m*m); best_b=b.copy()
    N=next_pow2(3*n-2)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    beta=0.35; temp=0.004; stale=0
    recompute_every=16 if n>8192 else 24
    print start
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        cmax=float(np.max(c))
        if cmax < best_max - 1e-12:
            best_max=cmax; best_score=...; best_b=b.copy(); print optional
        if cmax <= 0: break
        score_current = 2*n*cmax/(m*m)
        tau=beta*max(cmax,1e-12)
        z=(c-cmax)/tau; np.clip(z,-40,0,out=z); ez=np.exp(z); s=ez.sum()
        if finite: p=ez/s else p=zeros
        p = 0.98*p + 0.02/(2*n-1)
        fb=rfft(b,N); fp=rfft(p[::-1],N); cross=irfft(fb*fp,N)[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
        val=2*cross + p[0:2*n-1:2]
        moves=0
        while moves < recompute_every and time.monotonic() < deadline:
            ones=np.flatnonzero(b>0.5); zeros=np.flatnonzero(b<0.5)
            if ones.size<1 or zeros.size<1: break
            # 2-swap escape
            if stale > 150 and ones.size>=2 and zeros.size>=2 and rng.random() < 0.2:
                i1,i2=pick_two_unique(ones,rng); j1,j2=pick_two_unique(zeros,rng)
                if i1>=0 and j1>=0 and i1!=i2 and j1!=j2:

                    else: delta=... accept = rng.random() < np.exp(-delta/temp) if delta<5*temp else False
                    if accept:
                        score_current=score_try; if score_try<best_score: best...
                        stale=0; beta=max(0.07,beta*0.98); temp=min(0.02,temp*1.05)
                        moves = recompute_every # force recompute
                    else:
                        revert_kswap(...)
                        stale=0
                    continue
            # single swap choose
            if stale > 300 or rng.random() < 0.12:
                i=int(ones[rng.integers(ones.size)]); j=int(zeros[rng.integers(zeros.size)])
            else:
                if stale > 15:
                    vptp=float(np.ptp(val))
                    noise = 0.2 * max(1e-12, vptp) * min(1.0, stale/50.0)
                    vone = val[ones] + rng.normal(0.0, noise, ones.size)
                    vzero = val[zeros] + rng.normal(0.0, noise, zeros.size)
                else:
                    vone = val[ones]; vzero=val[zeros]
                i = int(ones[int(np.argmax(vone))])
                j = int(zeros[int(np.argmin(vzero))])
            if i == j: continue
            # apply single
            c[i:i+n] -= 2.0*b; c[2*i] += 1.0; b[i]=0.0
            c[j:j+n] += 2.0*b; c[2*j] += 1.0; b[j]=1.0

                c[i:i+n] += 2.0*b; c[2*i] += 1.0; b[i]=1.0

def next_prime(x):
    x=int(max(3,x))
    while not is_prime(x): x+=1
    return x

Let's write `logit_score_grad_beta` top-level:
```
def logit_score_grad_beta(z, n, N, beta):
    a = softmax_seq(z)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, N)[:2*n-1]
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    if cmax <= 0:
       exact=0; p=np.full(2*n-1,1/...)
       grad=zeros
       return exact, cmax, grad
    tau = beta*max(cmax,1e-300)
    shifted=(c-cmax)/tau
    np.clip(shifted,-40,0,out=shifted)
    ez=np.exp(shifted); s=ez.sum()
    if finite: p=ez/s else uniform
    p = 0.98*p +0.02/(2*n-1)
    p_rev=p[::-1]; fw=rfft(p_rev,N); cross=irfft(fa*fw,N)[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
    G=4.0*n*cross
    total=float(a.dot(G))
    grad_z = a*(G - total)
    exact = float(2.0*n*cmax)
    return exact, cmax, grad_z
```
Actually dJ/da for exact max subgradient uses p indicator? Our p softmax. Fine.

If beta small, tau maybe 0? Use max.

def optimize_logit(a0, deadline, seed, verbose=False):
    a=normalize_positive(a0,1e-12)
    n=a.size
    if n<2 or time...: return a, internal_score
    N=next_pow2(3*n-2)
    z=np.log(a); z-=z.mean(); z=np.clip(z,-25,25)
    best_a=a.copy(); best_score=internal_score(a,N)
    factor= None
    rng=default_rng(seed)
    lr=0.02; b1=0.8; b2=0.95; eps=1e-8
    m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n); t=0; no_imp=0
    start_best=best_score
    while time < deadline - 5s:
        exact, cmax, g = logit_score_grad_beta(z,n,N,0.25)
        if exact < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score=exact; best_a=softmax_seq(z).copy(); no_imp=0; print
        # update
        m = b1*m + (1-b1)*g
        v = b2*v + (1-b2)*g*g
        t +=1
        mhat = m/(1-b1**t); vhat=v/(1-b2**t)
        step = lr * mhat/(np.sqrt(vhat)+eps)
        # clip step norm
        step_norm = np.linalg.norm(step)
        if step_norm>1.0: step*=1.0/step_norm
        z_new = z - step
        z_new -= z_new.mean(); z_new=np.clip(z_new,-25,25)
        a_new=softmax_seq(z_new)
        c_new=convolve_full(a_new,N)  # uses N maybe; exact cmax
        exact_new=float(2*n*np.max(c_new))
        if exact_new < exact - 1e-12:
            z=z_new; exact=exact_new
            lr=min(0.15, lr*1.04)
            if exact < best_score: best...
        else:
            # try half? optional to reduce rejections:
            z_half = z - 0.5*step; z_half clip; a_half... exact_half
            if exact_half < exact:
                z=z_half; exact=exact_half; lr=min(0.15,lr*1.0)
            else:
                lr=max(1e-4, lr*0.8)
                no_imp +=1
                if no_imp > 400:
                    # perturb and reset? 
                    z = z + rng.normal(0.0,0.15,n)
                    z -= z.mean(); z=clip
                    m.fill(0); v.fill(0); t=0; no_imp=0
                    lr=0.03
        if verbose and t%500==0: print
        if best_score < 1.05: break? maybe not.
    return best_a, best_score
```

Let's estimate more realistically: In Python, slice multiply `c[i:i+n] -= 2.0 * b` creates temporary `2.0*b` length n (16k) and does in-place subtract. Two such per move: 32k multiplications. `np.max(c)` length 32767. `flatnonzero` length 16k. Argmax on val slice length m 8k and zero 8k. Total maybe 100k scalar operations in C plus several numpy allocations. Numpy allocation 16k ~128KB; per move maybe 0.1-0.2 ms. 1000 moves=0.2s. 100k moves=20s. 1000s can do 5M moves? That might be too many but still okay? Actually 5M*0.2ms=1000s; yes. Memory allocations repeated could be heavy. It may optimize a lot. But maybe too much, but fine. However each recompute every 16 moves: FFT length 65536 length 2 transforms. If 5M moves -> 312k recomputes -> 624k transforms, likely hours. But deadline stops; per recompute maybe 0.5ms -> 156s for 312k? Wait if 5M moves /16 =312k recomputes; 2 transforms each maybe 0.2ms ->125s; not too bad? Plus p construction O(n) 312k*16k=5G ops -> maybe seconds. Could be heavy. But in 1000s, maybe 100k moves, not 5M. Because per move >0.2ms? Let's not overestimate. If 1000s, even 50k moves enough maybe. But if moves are fast, it may run many and use all time; okay. It will return best by deadline.

Potential risk: binary optimize with `np.flatnonzero` and `np.argmax` each move plus recompute if very fast could use huge CPU but within. Good.

But if it runs 1000s, leftover logit small. That's okay if binary best improved.

Maybe scales: In optimize, inner moves only `recompute_every` many between recomputes; even if each move 0.05ms, per recompute block 16 moves + recompute (FFT+flat) maybe recompute 0.5ms, total 1.3ms; in 1000s ~770k blocks? No 1000/0.0013=770k blocks -> 12M moves; too much. But recompute FFT 65536 maybe 0.5ms? 2 transforms ~1ms. Then block 1ms + moves0.8ms=1.8ms; 1000s=555k blocks ->8.9M moves. That might be too many if FFT 0.5ms impossible? 1e8 elements processed, maybe seconds? Hard. But even if 1M moves, okay. Need not control max moves. The objective may keep improving slightly; no issue.

But if binary optimize uses too much time and logit none, target met. Fine.

Potential issue: If optimize_binary gets into a cycle accepting SA moves with equal score, m can change? m fixed. It could wander, best unchanged. But deadline passes return raw. okay.

Now, ensure final `best_a` and `best_score` update from optimize_binary. `optimize_binary` returns normalized best_b and exact binary score. But if best_a was continuous parent and binary score better, update. If binary score not finite, skip.

One conder: The raw binary candidate scores sorted by binary_score using c rint. But logit internal score may define `best_score` with continuous. Use same scoring? We can update best_score with returned exact binary. If logit returns `internal_score` exact. Good.

Now, let's consider if a raw QR has score 1.51 but random 1.15, selection top random. Good.

Let's test small update functions in optimize `revert` after single:
After forward, b[i]=0,b[j]=1. Revert:
```
c[j:j+n] -= 2.0*b
c[2*j] += 1.0
b[j]=0.0
c[i:i+n] += 2.0*b
c[2*i] += 1.0
b[i]=1.0
```
This uses b with i=0,j=1 for remove j: subtract pairs involving j in current support (all old except i plus j). It includes pair j? self subtract? `2*b` includes b_j=1 -> at 2j subtract2, +1 => -1, correct. It subtracts cross with all other current support. Then set j=0. Add i: current support old without i,j. Add pairs with all, including j=0. Good. Restores. If i and j slices overlap, linear operations okay. Good.

2-swap revert uses apply/revert functions; check with generic remove/add sequences; should restore.

Now, what about `np.max(c)` when after 2-swap apply all removes before adds, intermediate c after removes may have negative values? c represents support missing some ones; nonnegative for that support? If we apply removes sequentially, after remove all, c is convolution of support R_removed, nonnegative. After adds, final nonnegative. During apply, before all adds but after some adds, c is convolution of intermediate support, nonnegative. Good. So max trial correct. No negative temporary. Good.

But in apply_kswap, if we remove a set of ones, `c` after each removal is convolution of current support, nonnegative. Good.

Now, let's decide `recompute_every`: If n=16384, 8 maybe. If 16, p stale. Use `recompute_every = 8 if n > 8192 else 12`. More recomputes but better. 1000s maybe okay. Let's set 10 for large, 16 small. Or dynamic: if time left. Use 12 all. Simpler: `recompute_every = 12 if n > 4096 else 20`. Good.

Now, if raw best score already <1.3, binary optimize maybe not necessary, but code will improve. Good.

Let's write each function in detail.

`next_pow2`:
```
def next_pow2(m: int) -> int:
    m = int(max(1, m))
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()
```

`normalize_positive`:
```
def normalize_positive(a, floor=0.0):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
        a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    if floor > 0.0:
        a = np.maximum(a, float(floor))
    else:
        a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(a.size, 1.0 / float(a.size), dtype=np.float64)
    return a / s
```

`softmax_seq`:
```
def softmax_seq(z):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if z.size == 0: return np.zeros(0)
    z = z - float(np.max(z))
    z = np.clip(z, -60.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(z)
    e = np.maximum(e, 1e-300)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(z.size, 1.0/float(z.size))
    return e / s
```

`convolve_full`:
```
def convolve_full(a, N=None):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if N is None:
        N = next_pow2(2*n - 1)
    else:
        need = next_pow2(2*n - 1)
        if int(N) < int(need): N = need
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, N)[:2*n - 1]
    return c.astype(np.float64, copy=False)
```

`internal_score`:
```
def internal_score(a, N=None):
    a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return inf
    a=normalize_positive(a,0.0)
    c=convolve_full(a,N)
    cmax=float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(cmax): return inf
    return float(2.0*n*cmax)
```

`binary_score`:
```
def binary_score(b):
    b=np.asarray(b,float).ravel()
    n=b.size
    m=float(np.rint(np.sum(b)))  # if b has nonbinary? sum
    if m < 1.0: return inf,0
    if m > float(n): m=float(n)
    c=convolve_full(b)
    # for exactness if b nearly binary
    if np.all((b <= 0.5) | (b >= 0.5)):
        c=np.rint(c)
    cmax=float(np.max(c))
    if not finite: return inf
    if m <= 0: return inf
    return float(2.0*n*cmax/(m*m)), cmax
```
If b has fractional values from continuous top_threshold? We only use binary. For safety if fractional, no rint, m fractional. But score formula scale? c total=m^2? If a values not 0/1 but sum m, linear homogeneous, score same using sum m. Good. If fractional, m=sum.

`consider_binary_raw` top-level:
```
def consider_binary_raw(raw_list, b, kind):
    b=np.asarray(b,float).ravel()
    m=float(b.sum())
    if m < 2.0 or m >= float(b.size): # if all ones maybe skip? all ones score 2, not useful but maybe length small? skip.
        pass not return? If m all ones but might still be valid; not chosen. Let's allow if m>=1.
    sc, cm = binary_score(b)
    if np.isfinite(sc) and m >= 2 and m <= b.size - 1:
        raw_list.append((sc, b.copy(), len(b), kind))
```
If m=1 or n, score maybe high; skip as raw option.

`top_m_binary`:
```
def top_m_binary(a, m):
    a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel(); n=a.size
    m=int(max(0,min(n,m)))
    b=np.zeros(n,float)
    if m<=0: return b
    if m>=n: b.fill(1.0); return b
    order=np.argpartition(-a, m-1)[:m]
    b[order]=1.0
    return b
```
If a has NaNs? nan_to_num.

`is_prime` and `next_prime` as above.

`qr_binary`:
```
def qr_binary(p, include_zero=True):
    p=int(p)
    x=np.arange(p,dtype=np.int64)
    idx=(x*x)%p
    b=np.zeros(p,float); b[idx]=1.0
    if not include_zero: b[0]=0.0
    return b
```

`make_random_binary`:
```
def make_random_binary(n, density, rng):
    n=int(max(2,n)); m=int(round(float(n)*float(density)))
    b=np.zeros(n,float)
    if m<=0: return b
    if m>=n: b.fill(1.0); return b
    r=rng.random(n)
    idx=np.argpartition(r, m-1)[:m]
    b[idx]=1.0
    return b
```
For m maybe n-1, argpartition r with m-1 ok.

`remove_one`, `add_one`, `apply_kswap`, `revert_kswap`, `pick_two_unique`.

`pick_two_unique`:
```
def pick_two_unique(pool, rng):
    m=pool.size
    if m < 2:
        return -1, -1
    a=int(rng.integers(m))
    b=int(rng.integers(m-1))
    if b >= a:
        b += 1
    return int(pool[a]), int(pool[b])
```

`cand_key`:
```
def cand_key(item):
    return item[0]
```

`optimize_binary`: Need code with `time.monotonic` repeated. For verbose prints, include n, score maybe not too frequent. We can print when best improves. But if many improvements, output huge. Use if verbose and improvement count %? We can print first few. Simpler: print when best improves and improvement > 1e-4 or first 20. Keep small.

Implement:
```
def optimize_binary(b0, deadline, seed=0, verbose=False):
    b = np.asarray(b0, dtype=np.float64).ravel().copy()
    n = b.size
    if n < 2: return ...
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
    m = int(np.rint(float(np.sum(b))))
    if m < 2 or m > n-2:
       b = make_random_binary(n, 0.5, rng)
       m = int(np.rint(b.sum()))
    b = np.where(b > 0.5, 1.0, 0.0)
    # If due rounding count mismatch, adjust using top_m_binary from b? For binary, if sum mismatch, use top_m_binary(b, m)
    if int(np.rint(b.sum())) != m:
        b = top_m_binary(b, m)
    Nc = next_pow2(2*n - 1)
    c = np.rint(convolve_full(b, Nc)).astype(np.float64)
    m2 = float(m)*float(m)
    factor = 2.0*float(n)/m2
    best_max = float(np.max(c))
    best_score = float(factor*best_max)
    best_b = b.copy()
    N = next_pow2(3*n - 2)
    beta=0.35; temp=0.004; stale=0; print_count=0
    recompute_every = 10 if n > 8192 else 16
    if verbose: print("binary start n={} m={} score={:.9f}".format(n,m,best_score), flush=True)
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        cmax = float(np.max(c))
        if cmax < best_max - 1e-12:
            best_max=cmax; best_score=factor*best_max; best_b=b.copy()
            if verbose:
                print_count +=1
                if print_count <= 50 or print_count % 1000 == 0:
                    print("binary n={} improved best={:.9f}".format(n,best_score), flush=True)
        if cmax <= 0 or not np.isfinite(cmax): break
        score_current = factor*cmax
        tau = beta*max(cmax, 1e-12)
        shifted = (c - cmax) / tau
        np.clip(shifted, -40.0, 0.0, out=shifted)
        ez = np.exp(shifted)
        s = float(ez.sum())
        L = 2*n - 1
        if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
            p = np.full(L, 1.0/float(L))
        else:
            p = ez / s
        p = 0.98*p + 0.02/float(L)
        fb = np.fft.rfft(b, N)
        fw = np.fft.rfft(p[::-1], N)
        cross = np.fft.irfft(fb * fw, N)[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
        val = 2.0*cross + p[0:L:2]
        moves=0
        while moves < recompute_every and time.monotonic() < deadline:
            ones = np.flatnonzero(b > 0.5)
            zeros = np.flatnonzero(b < 0.5)
            if ones.size < 1 or zeros.size < 1: break
            if stale > 150 and ones.size >= 2 and zeros.size >= 2 and rng.random() < 0.25:
                top_size = min(12, ones.size)
                if top_size >= 2:
                    top_one_loc = np.argpartition(-val[ones], top_size-1)[:top_size]
                    top_zero_loc = np.argpartition(val[zeros], top_size-1)[:top_size]
                    top_one = ones[top_one_loc]
                    top_zero = zeros[top_zero_loc]
                    i1,i2 = pick_two_unique(top_one, rng)
                    j1,j2 = pick_two_unique(top_zero, rng)
                else:
                    i1,i2,j1,j2 = -1,-1,-1,-1
                if i1 >= 0 and j1 >= 0 and i1 != i2 and j1 != j2:
                    apply_kswap(b, c, (i1,i2), (j1,j2))
                    cmax_try = float(np.max(c))
                    score_try = factor*cmax_try
                    if score_try < score_current*(1.0 - 1e-12):
                        accept = True
                    else:
                        delta = score_try - score_current
                        if delta <= 1e-13:
                            accept = rng.random() < 0.3
                        else:
                            accept = rng.random() < np.exp(-delta/temp) if delta < 5.0*temp else False
                    if accept:
                        score_current = score_try
                        if score_try < best_score:
                            best_score=score_try; best_max=cmax_try; best_b=b.copy()
                            if verbose: print...
                        stale=0; beta=max(0.06,beta*0.98); temp=min(0.02,temp*1.05)
                        moves = recompute_every
                    else:
                        revert_kswap(b, c, (i1,i2), (j1,j2))
                        stale=0
                    continue
            # single move
            if stale > 300 or rng.random() < 0.10:
                i = int(ones[int(rng.integers(ones.size))])
                j = int(zeros[int(rng.integers(zeros.size))])
            else:
                if stale > 15:
                    vptp = float(np.ptp(val))
                    noise = 0.2 * max(1e-12, vptp) * min(1.0, float(stale)/50.0)
                    vone = val[ones] + rng.normal(0.0, noise, ones.size)
                    vzero = val[zeros] + rng.normal(0.0, noise, zeros.size)
                else:
                    vone = val[ones]
                    vzero = val[zeros]
                i = int(ones[int(np.argmax(vone))])
                j = int(zeros[int(np.argmin(vzero))])
            if i == j:
                continue
            # forward
            c[i:i+n] -= 2.0*b
            c[2*i] += 1.0
            b[i] = 0.0
            c[j:j+n] += 2.0*b
            c[2*j] += 1.0
            b[j] = 1.0
            cmax_new = float(np.max(c))
            score_new = factor*cmax_new
            if score_new < score_current*(1.0 - 1e-12):
                accept = True
            else:
                delta = score_new - score_current
                if delta <= 1e-13:
                    accept = rng.random() < 0.25
                else:
                    accept = rng.random() < np.exp(-delta/temp) if delta < 5.0*temp else False
            if accept:
                score_current = score_new
                if score_new < best_score:
                    best_score = score_new; best_max = cmax_new; best_b = b.copy()
                    if verbose: print...
                stale=0
                beta = max(0.06, beta*0.985)
                temp = min(0.02, temp*1.03)
                moves += 1
            else:
                # revert
                c[j:j+n] -= 2.0*b
                c[2*j] += 1.0
                b[j] = 0.0
                c[i:i+n] += 2.0*b
                c[2*i] += 1.0
                b[i] = 1.0
                stale += 1
                temp = max(1e-5, temp*0.97)
                if stale % 80 == 0:
                    beta = min(1.2, beta*1.3)
                    temp = min(0.04, temp*1.5)
                    stale = 0
                moves += 1
    best_a = normalize_positive(best_b, 0.0)
    # maybe exact final score using internal to avoid binary factor mismatch?
    # return best_a, best_score
    return best_a, best_score
```

`optimize_logit` implement as top-level:
```
def optimize_logit(a0, deadline, seed, verbose=False):
    a = normalize_positive(a0, 1e-12)
    n = a.size
    if n < 4 or time.monotonic() > deadline - 10:
        return a, internal_score(a)
    N = next_pow2(3*n - 2)
    z = np.log(a)
    z -= float(np.mean(z))
    z = np.clip(z, -25.0, 25.0)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_score = internal_score(a, N)
    if not np.isfinite(best_score): best_score = inf
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
    lr = 0.025
    b1 = 0.80
    b2 = 0.95
    eps = 1e-8
    mmat = np.zeros(n)
    vmat = np.zeros(n)
    t = 0
    no_imp = 0
    if verbose: print(...)
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 2.0:
        exact, cmax, g = logit_score_grad_beta(z, n, N, 0.25)
        if not np.isfinite(exact): break
        if exact < best_score - 1e-12:
            # need a corresponding to z
            a_now = softmax_seq(z)
            best_score = exact
            best_a = a_now.copy()
            no_imp = 0
            if verbose and (t < 10 or t % 1000 == 0): print...
        # update smoothing/Adam
        mmat = b1*mmat + (1.0-b1)*g
        vmat = b2*vmat + (1.0-b2)*(g*g)
        t += 1
        b1_t = 1.0 - b1**t
        b2_t = 1.0 - b2**t
        mhat = mmat/b1_t
        vhat = vmat/b2_t
        step = lr * mhat/(np.sqrt(vhat)+eps)
        step_norm = float(np.linalg.norm(step))
        if not np.isfinite(step_norm) or step_norm <= 0: continue
        if step_norm > 1.0:
            step *= (1.0/step_norm)
        z_new = z - step
        z_new -= float(np.mean(z_new))
        z_new = np.clip(z_new, -25.0, 25.0)
        # trial exact
        a_new = softmax_seq(z_new)
        c_try = convolve_full(a_new, N)
        cmax_try = float(np.max(c_try))
        exact_try = float(2.0*n*cmax_try)
        accepted = False
        if np.isfinite(exact_try) and exact_try < exact - 1e-12:
            z = z_new
            exact = exact_try
            accepted = True
            lr = min(0.15, lr*1.03)
            if exact < best_score - 1e-12:
                best_score=exact; best_a=a_new.copy(); no_imp=0
                if verbose...
        else:
            # half step
            z_half = z - 0.5*step
            z_half -= mean; z_half clip
            a_half = softmax_seq(z_half)
            c_half = convolve_full(a_half, N)
            exact_half = 2n max
            if np.isfinite(exact_half) and exact_half < exact - 1e-12:
                z = z_half; exact=exact_half; accepted=True
                if exact < best_score: update
            else:
                lr = max(1e-4, lr*0.85)
                no_imp += 1
                if no_imp > 600:
                    z = z + rng.normal(0.0, 0.12, n)
                    z -= mean; clip
                    mmat.fill(0.0); vmat.fill(0.0); t=0; no_imp=0; lr=0.035
        if no_imp > 4000:
            break
    return best_a, best_score
```
This may be a bit heavy. Could simplify by not doing half step to save time. Let's remove half step maybe; just if trial worsens reduce lr. But if lr fixed too high, stuck. Use scalar step clip 1.0; maybe okay. Let's remove half to cut Fourier evaluations. Use only trial. Adam with line search no half. If no improve, reduce. Fine.

Now, `propose_candidate` full:
```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    start=time.monotonic()

def resample_seq(a, n):
    a=normalize_positive(a,0.0)

"""
Search for non-negative step-function heights minimizing

    2 * n * max(convolve(a, a)) / sum(a)^2

by exploiting:
  - large 0/1 sequences with random and quadratic-residue supports,
  - exact O(n) swap updates for binary self-convolution,
  - entropy-softmax weighted gradient guidance for binary local search,
  - a light log-softmaxAdam / smooth-max polishing phase for continuous values.

The best sequence found before the deadline is returned.
"""

import time
import numpy as np


def next_pow2(m: int) -> int:
    m = int(max(1, m))
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def normalize_positive(a, floor: float = 0.0) -> np.ndarray:
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    if floor > 0.0:
        a = np.maximum(a, float(floor))
    else:
        a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(a.size, 1.0 / float(a.size), dtype=np.float64)
    return a / s


def softmax_seq(z) -> np.ndarray:
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if z.size == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    z = z - float(np.max(z))
    z = np.clip(z, -60.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(z)
    e = np.maximum(e, 1e-300)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(z.size, 1.0 / float(z.size), dtype=np.float64)
    return e / s


def convolve_full(a, N=None) -> np.ndarray:
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if N is None:
        N = next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    else:
        need = next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
        if int(N) < int(need):
            N = need
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, N)[:2 * n - 1]
    return c.astype(np.float64, copy=False)


def internal_score(a, N=None) -> float:
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    a = normalize_positive(a, 0.0)
    c = convolve_full(a, N)
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(cmax):
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * n * cmax)


def binary_score(b):
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = b.size
    m = float(np.sum(b))
    if m < 1.0 or not np.isfinite(m):
        return float("inf"), 0.0
    c = convolve_full(b)
    b_is_binary = np.all((b <= 0.5) | (b >= 0.5))
    if b_is_binary:
        c = np.rint(c)
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(cmax):
        return float("inf"), 0.0
    return float(2.0 * n * cmax / (m * m)), cmax


def resample_seq(a, n: int) -> np.ndarray:
    a = normalize_positive(a, 0.0)
    old_n = a.size
    n = int(max(2, n))
    if old_n == n:
        return a.astype(np.float64, copy=True)
    if old_n == 1:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
    old_x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, int(old_n), dtype=np.float64)
    new_x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    b = np.interp(new_x, old_x, a)
    return normalize_positive(b, 1e-12)


def top_m_binary(a, m: int) -> np.ndarray:
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    n = a.size
    m = int(max(0, min(n, m)))
    b = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if m <= 0:
        return b
    if m >= n:
        b.fill(1.0)
        return b
    order = np.argmax(a) if m == 1 else np.argpartition(-a, m - 1)[:m]
    b[order] = 1.0
    return b


def is_prime(x: int) -> bool:
    x = int(x)
    if x < 2:
        return False
    if x % 2 == 0:
        return x == 2
    if x % 3 == 0:
        return x == 3
    d = 5
    w = 2
    while d * d <= x:
        if x % d == 0:
            return False
        d += w
        w = 6 - w
    return True


def next_prime(x: int) -> int:
    x = int(max(3, x))
    while not is_prime(x):
        x += 1
    return x


def qr_binary(p: int, include_zero: bool = True) -> np.ndarray:
    p = int(max(3, p))
    x = np.arange(p, dtype=np.int64)
    idx = (x * x) % p
    b = np.zeros(p, dtype=np.float64)
    b[idx] = 1.0
    if not include_zero:
        b[0] = 0.0
    return b


def make_random_binary(n: int, density: float, rng) -> np.ndarray:
    n = int(max(2, n))
    m = int(round(float(n) * float(density)))
    b = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if m <= 0:
        return b
    if m >= n:
        b.fill(1.0)
        return b
    r = rng.random(n)
    idx = np.argpartition(r, m - 1)[:m]
    b[idx] = 1.0
    return b


def consider_binary_raw(raw_list, b, kind: str) -> None:
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = b.size
    m = float(np.sum(b))
    if n < 4 or m < 2.0 or m >= float(n):
        return
    sc, _ = binary_score(b)
    if np.isfinite(sc):
        raw_list.append((sc, b.copy(), n, kind))


def cand_key(item):
    return item[0]


def remove_one(b, c, i: int) -> None:
    n = b.size
    c[i:i + n] -= 2.0 * b
    c[2 * i] += 1.0
    b[i] = 0.0


def add_one(b, c, j: int) -> None:
    n = b.size
    c[j:j + n] += 2.0 * b
    c[2 * j] += 1.0
    b[j] = 1.0


def apply_kswap(b, c, removes, adds) -> None:
    n = b.size
    for i in removes:
        if b[int(i)] > 0.5:
            c[i:i + n] -= 2.0 * b
            c[2 * i] += 1.0
            b[i] = 0.0
    for j in adds:
        if b[int(j)] < 0.5:
            c[j:j + n] += 2.0 * b
            c[2 * j] += 1.0
            b[j] = 1.0


def revert_kswap(b, c, removes, adds) -> None:
    n = b.size
    for j in reversed(adds):
        if b[int(j)] > 0.5:
            c[j:j + n] -= 2.0 * b
            c[2 * j] += 1.0
            b[j] = 0.0
    for i in reversed(removes):
        if b[int(i)] < 0.5:
            c[i:i + n] += 2.0 * b
            c[2 * i] += 1.0
            b[i] = 1.0


def pick_two_unique(pool, rng):
    m = pool.size
    if m < 2:
        return -1, -1
    a = int(rng.integers(m))
    b = int(rng.integers(m - 1))
    if b >= a:
        b += 1
    return int(pool[a]), int(pool[b])


def optimize_binary(b0, deadline: float, seed: int = 0, verbose: bool = False):
    b = np.asarray(b0, dtype=np.float64).ravel().copy()
    n = b.size
    if n < 4:
        b = np.ones(64, dtype=np.float64) / 64.0
        return b, internal_score(b)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
    m = int(np.rint(float(np.sum(b))))
    if m < 2 or m > n - 2:
        b = make_random_binary(n, 0.5, rng)
        m = int(np.rint(b.sum()))
    b = np.where(b > 0.5, 1.0, 0.0)

    if int(np.rint(b.sum())) != m:
        b = top_m_binary(b, m)
        m = int(np.rint(b.sum()))
    if m < 2 or m > n - 2:
        b = make_random_binary(n, 0.5, rng)
        m = int(np.rint(b.sum()))

    c = np.rint(convolve_full(b, next_pow2(2 * n - 1))).astype(np.float64)
    m2 = float(m) * float(m)
    factor = 2.0 * float(n) / m2
    best_max = float(np.max(c))
    best_score = float(factor * best_max)
    best_b = b.copy()

    N = next_pow2(3 * n - 2)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    beta = 0.35
    temp = 0.004
    stale = 0
    recompute_every = 10 if n > 8192 else 16
    print_count = 0

    if verbose:
        print("binary start n={} m={} score={:.9f}".format(n, m, best_score), flush=True)

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        cmax = float(np.max(c))
        if cmax < best_max - 1e-12:
            best_max = cmax
            best_score = float(factor * best_max)
            best_b = b.copy()
            if verbose:
                print_count += 1
                if print_count <= 60 or print_count % 500 == 0:
                    print("binary n={} improved best={:.9f}".format(n, best_score), flush=True)

        if cmax <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(cmax):
            break

        score_current = float(factor * cmax)

        tau = beta * max(cmax, 1e-12)
        shifted = (c - cmax) / tau
        np.clip(shifted, -40.0, 0.0, out=shifted)
        ez = np.exp(shifted)
        s = float(ez.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
            p = np.full(L, 1.0 / float(L), dtype=np.float64)
        else:
            p = ez / s
        p = 0.98 * p + 0.02 / float(L)

        fb = np.fft.rfft(b, N)
        fw = np.fft.rfft(p[::-1], N)
        cross = np.fft.irfft(fb * fw, N)[n - 1:2 * n - 1][::-1]
        val = 2.0 * cross + p[0:L:2]

        moves = 0
        while moves < recompute_every and time.monotonic() < deadline:
            ones = np.flatnonzero(b > 0.5)
            zeros = np.flatnonzero(b < 0.5)
            if ones.size < 1 or zeros.size < 1:
                break

            did_escape = False
            if stale > 150 and ones.size >= 2 and zeros.size >= 2 and rng.random() < 0.25:
                ti = min(12, int(ones.size))
                tz = min(12, int(zeros.size))
                if ti >= 2 and tz >= 2:
                    top_one_loc = np.argpartition(-val[ones], ti - 1)[:ti]
                    top_zero_loc = np.argpartition(val[zeros], tz - 1)[:tz]
                    top_one = ones[top_one_loc]
                    top_zero = zeros[top_zero_loc]
                    i1, i2 = pick_two_unique(top_one, rng)
                    j1, j2 = pick_two_unique(top_zero, rng)
                    if i1 >= 0 and j1 >= 0 and i1 != i2 and j1 != j2:
                        did_escape = True
                        apply_kswap(b, c, (i1, i2), (j1, j2))
                        cmax_try = float(np.max(c))
                        score_try = float(factor * cmax_try)
                        if score_try < score_current * (1.0 - 1e-12):
                            accept_escape = True
                        else:
                            delta = score_try - score_current
                            if delta <= 1e-13:
                                accept_escape = rng.random() < 0.3
                            else:
                                accept_escape = rng.random() < np.exp(-delta / temp) if delta < 5.0 * temp else False
                        if accept_escape:
                            score_current = score_try
                            if score_try < best_score:
                                best_score = score_try
                                best_max = cmax_try
                                best_b = b.copy()
                                if verbose:
                                    print_count += 1
                                    if print_count <= 60 or print_count % 500 == 0:
                                        print("binary n={} improved best={:.9f}".format(n, best_score), flush=True)
                            stale = 0
                            beta = max(0.06, beta * 0.98)
                            temp = min(0.02, temp * 1.05)
                            moves = recompute_every
                        else:
                            revert_kswap(b, c, (i1, i2), (j1, j2))
                            stale = 0

            if did_escape:
                continue

            if stale > 300 or rng.random() < 0.10:
                i = int(ones[int(rng.integers(ones.size))])
                j = int(zeros[int(rng.integers(zeros.size))])
            else:
                if stale > 15:
                    vptp = float(np.ptp(val))
                    noise = 0.2 * max(1e-12, vptp) * min(1.0, float(stale) / 50.0)
                    vone = val[ones] + rng.normal(0.0, noise, ones.size)
                    vzero = val[zeros] + rng.normal(0.0, noise, zeros.size)
                else:
                    vone = val[ones]
                    vzero = val[zeros]
                i = int(ones[int(np.argmax(vone))])
                j = int(zeros[int(np.argmin(vzero))])

            if i == j:
                continue

            c[i:i + n] -= 2.0 * b
            c[2 * i] += 1.0
            b[i] = 0.0

            c[j:j + n] += 2.0 * b
            c[2 * j] += 1.0
            b[j] = 1.0

            cmax_new = float(np.max(c))
            score_new = float(factor * cmax_new)

            if score_new < score_current * (1.0 - 1e-12):
                accept = True
            else:
                delta = score_new - score_current
                if delta <= 1e-13:
                    accept = rng.random() < 0.25
                else:
                    accept = rng.random() < np.exp(-delta / temp) if delta < 5.0 * temp else False

            if accept:
                score_current = score_new
                if score_new < best_score:
                    best_score = score_new
                    best_max = cmax_new
                    best_b = b.copy()
                    if verbose:
                        print_count += 1
                        if print_count <= 60 or print_count % 500 == 0:
                            print("binary n={} improved best={:.9f}".format(n, best_score), flush=True)
                stale = 0
                beta = max(0.06, beta * 0.985)
                temp = min(0.02, temp * 1.03)
                moves += 1
            else:
                c[j:j + n] -= 2.0 * b
                c[2 * j] += 1.0
                b[j] = 0.0

                c[i:i + n] += 2.0 * b
                c[2 * i] += 1.0
                b[i] = 1.0

                stale += 1
                temp = max(1e-5, temp * 0.97)
                if stale % 80 == 0:
                    beta = min(1.2, beta * 1.3)
                    temp = min(0.04, temp * 1.5)
                    stale = 0
                moves += 1

    best_a = normalize_positive(best_b, 0.0)
    return best_a, float(best_score)


def logit_score_grad_beta(z, n: int, N: int, beta: float):
    a = softmax_seq(z)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, N)[:2 * n - 1]
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    L = 2 * n - 1
    if cmax <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(cmax):
        exact = 0.0
        p = np.full(L, 1.0 / float(L), dtype=np.float64)
        grad = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        return exact, cmax, grad

    tau = float(beta) * max(cmax, 1e-300)
    shifted = (c - cmax) / tau
    np.clip(shifted, -40.0, 0.0, out=shifted)
    ez = np.exp(shifted)
    s = float(ez.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        p = np.full(L, 1.0 / float(L), dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        p = ez / s
    p = 0.98 * p + 0.02 / float(L)

    fw = np.fft.rfft(p[::-1], N)
    cross = np.fft.irfft(fa * fw, N)[n - 1:2 * n - 1][::-1]
    g_a = 4.0 * float(n) * cross
    total = float(a.dot(g_a))
    grad = a * (g_a - total)
    exact = float(2.0 * n * cmax)
    return exact, cmax, grad


def optimize_logit(a0, deadline: float, seed: int = 0, verbose: bool = False):
    a = normalize_positive(a0, 1e-12)
    n = a.size
    if n < 16 or time.monotonic() > deadline - 5.0:
        return a, internal_score(a)

    N = next_pow2(3 * n - 2)
    z = np.log(a)
    z -= float(np.mean(z))
    z = np.clip(z, -25.0, 25.0)

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_score = internal_score(a, N)
    if not np.isfinite(best_score):
        best_score = float("inf")

    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
    lr = 0.025
    b1 = 0.80
    b2 = 0.95
    eps = 1e-8
    mom = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    var = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    t = 0
    no_imp = 0
    print_it = 0

    if verbose:
        print("logit start n={} score={:.9f}".format(n, best_score), flush=True)

    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 2.0:
        exact, cmax, g = logit_score_grad_beta(z, n, N, 0.25)
        if not np.isfinite(exact):
            break

        if exact < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score = exact
            best_a = softmax_seq(z).copy()
            no_imp = 0
            if verbose:
                print_it += 1
                if print_it <= 20 or print_it % 500 == 0:
                    print("logit n={} improved best={:.9f}".format(n, best_score), flush=True)

        mom = b1 * mom + (1.0 - b1) * g
        var = b2 * var + (1.0 - b2) * (g * g)
        t += 1
        b1_hat = 1.0 - float(b1) ** t
        b2_hat = 1.0 - float(b2) ** t
        mhat = mom / b1_hat
        vhat = var / b2_hat
        step = lr * mhat / (np.sqrt(vhat) + eps)

        step_norm = float(np.linalg.norm(step))
        if not np.isfinite(step_norm) or step_norm <= 0.0:
            continue
        if step_norm > 1.0:
            step *= (1.0 / step_norm)

        z_new = z - step
        z_new -= float(np.mean(z_new))
        z_new = np.clip(z_new, -25.0, 25.0)

        a_new = softmax_seq(z_new)
        c_new = convolve_full(a_new, N)
        cmax_new = float(np.max(c_new))
        exact_new = float(2.0 * n * cmax_new)

        if np.isfinite(exact_new) and exact_new < exact - 1e-12:
            z = z_new
            exact = exact_new
            lr = min(0.15, lr * 1.03)
            if exact < best_score - 1e-12:
                best_score = exact
                best_a = a_new.copy()
                no_imp = 0
                if verbose:
                    print_it += 1
                    if print_it <= 20 or print_it % 500 == 0:
                        print("logit n={} improved best={:.9f}".format(n, best_score), flush=True)
        else:
            no_imp += 1
            lr = max(1e-4, lr * 0.85)
            if no_imp > 800:
                z = z + rng.normal(0.0, 0.12, n)
                z -= float(np.mean(z))
                z = np.clip(z, -25.0, 25.0)
                mom.fill(0.0)
                var.fill(0.0)
                t = 0
                no_imp = 0
                lr = 0.035

        if no_imp > 6000:
            break

    return best_a, float(best_score)


def propose_candidate(seed: int = 42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    start = time.monotonic()

    scheduled = 2400.0
    try:
        if budget_s is not None:
            scheduled = min(float(budget_s), 2400.0)
    except Exception:
        scheduled = 2400.0
    if not np.isfinite(scheduled) or scheduled < 120.0:
        scheduled = 2400.0

    total = max(120.0, min(scheduled - 50.0, 2350.0))
    deadline = start + total

    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
    raw = []
    best_a = None
    best_score = float("inf")

    given = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    if given is not None:
        try:
            a_in = np.asarray(given, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            a_in = np.nan_to_num(a_in, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
            a_in = np.maximum(a_in, 0.0)
            if a_in.size > 0 and float(a_in.sum()) > 0.0:
                if a_in.size > 20000:
                    a_in = resample_seq(a_in, 12000)
                a_in = normalize_positive(a_in, 0.0)
                sc = internal_score(a_in)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                    best_score = sc
                    best_a = a_in.copy()
                    print("given sequence score = {:.9f}".format(best_score), flush=True)

                for dens in (0.45, 0.50, 0.55):
                    bm = top_m_binary(a_in, int(np.rint(a_in.size * dens)))
                    consider_binary_raw(raw, bm, "parent_threshold")
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Random binary supports, which are often already very good for large n.
    for n in (8192, 16384):
        for dens in (0.50, 0.55, 0.60):
            for _ in range(2):
                b = make_random_binary(n, dens, rng)
                consider_binary_raw(raw, b, "random")

    # Quadratic-residue-like starts on primes near useful sizes.
    p_targets = (2053, 4099, 8191, 16381)
    for p_target in p_targets:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 1800.0:
            break
        p = next_prime(p_target)
        if p < 128 or p > 20000:
            continue

        base = qr_binary(p, include_zero=True)
        base_rev = base[::-1].copy()
        transforms = (
            ("qr_base", base),
            ("qr_comp", 1.0 - base),
            ("qr_rev", base_rev),
            ("qr_comp_rev", 1.0 - base_rev),
        )

        if p <= 8192:
            shifts = (0, p // 6, p // 4, p // 3, p // 2, p // 5, int((seed * 1000003) % p))
        else:
            shifts = (0, p // 4, p // 3, p // 2, p // 5)

        for kind, b0 in transforms:
            seen = set()
            for sh in shifts:
                sh = int(sh) % p
                if sh in seen:
                    continue
                seen.add(sh)
                if sh == 0:
                    br = b0.copy()
                else:
                    br = np.roll(b0, sh).copy()
                consider_binary_raw(raw, br, kind)

    if raw:
        raw.sort(key=cand_key)
        for item in raw[:min(10, len(raw))]:
            print("raw candidate score={:.9f} n={} kind={}".format(item[0], item[2], item[3]), flush=True)

        sc0, b0, _, _ = raw[0]
        if np.isfinite(sc0) and sc0 < best_score:
            best_score = sc0
            best_a = normalize_positive(b0, 0.0)
            print("binary raw best score = {:.9f}".format(best_score), flush=True)

    chosen_idx = []
    if raw:
        chosen_idx.append(0)
        for i, item in enumerate(raw):
            if i not in chosen_idx and item[3].startswith("qr"):
                chosen_idx.append(i)
                break
        for i, item in enumerate(raw):
            if i not in chosen_idx and item[3] == "random" and item[2] >= 8192:
                chosen_idx.append(i)
                break
        for i, item in enumerate(raw):
            if i not in chosen_idx and item[2] != raw[0][2]:
                chosen_idx.append(i)
                break
        chosen_idx = chosen_idx[:3]

    opt_deadline = max(time.monotonic() + 30.0, deadline - 200.0)
    rem = opt_deadline - time.monotonic()

    if chosen_idx and rem > 60.0:
        k = len(chosen_idx)
        now = time.monotonic()
        if k == 1:
            dls = [opt_deadline]
        elif k == 2:
            dls = [now + 0.55 * rem, opt_deadline]
        else:
            dls = [now + 0.50 * rem, now + 0.80 * rem, opt_deadline]

        for idx, dl in zip(chosen_idx, dls):
            if time.monotonic() > dl - 10.0:
                break
            item = raw[idx]
            b_start = item[1]
            a1, s1 = optimize_binary(b_start, dl, seed + 10000 + int(item[2]), verbose=True)
            if np.isfinite(s1) and s1 < best_score:
                best_score = s1
                best_a = normalize_positive(a1, 0.0)
                print("selected binary score = {:.9f} n={}".format(best_score, best_a.size), flush=True)

    logit_deadline = max(time.monotonic() + 20.0, deadline - 10.0)
    if best_a is not None and time.monotonic() < logit_deadline - 30.0:
        a2, s2 = optimize_logit(best_a, logit_deadline, seed + 987654, verbose=True)
        if np.isfinite(s2) and s2 < best_score:
            best_score = s2
            best_a = normalize_positive(a2, 0.0)
            print("selected logit score = {:.9f} n={}".format(best_score, best_a.size), flush=True)

    if best_a is None or not np.all(np.isfinite(best_a)) or best_a.size < 2 or best_score > 100.0:
        best_a = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64) / 1024.0
        best_score = internal_score(best_a)

    best_a = np.asarray(best_a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    best_a = np.nan_to_num(best_a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    best_a = np.maximum(best_a, 0.0)
    s = float(best_a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 0.01:
        best_a = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64) / 1024.0
        s = 1.0
    else:
        best_a = best_a / s

    out_arr = best_a.copy()
    pos_mask = out_arr > 0.0
    if np.any(pos_mask):
        pos_vals = out_arr[pos_mask]
        if pos_vals.size <= 0.98 * out_arr.size and np.allclose(pos_vals, pos_vals[0], rtol=1e-9, atol=1e-12):
            out_arr[pos_mask] = 1.0

    out_list = [float(x) for x in out_arr]

    try:
        final_eval = float(evaluate_sequence(out_list))
    except Exception:
        final_eval = float("inf")

    if not np.isfinite(final_eval):
        out_arr = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64) / 1024.0
        out_list = [float(x) for x in out_arr]
        try:
            final_eval = float(evaluate_sequence(out_list))
        except Exception:
            final_eval = 2.0

    print("final internal score = {:.12f}".format(float(best_score), flush=True))
    print("final evaluated score = {:.12f}, length = {}".format(final_eval, len(out_list)), flush=True)
    return out_list
```
